POCHUCK CREEK NEAR PINE ISLAND NY, USGS site 01369000 (USGS-01369000), stream in Orange County, New York, draining 98 square miles. Daily means from 1937-09-01 to 1977-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
